# Renamed overloads

Generated by com.lehman.aussomcraft.gen.Generator. Do not edit.

Aussom externs overload by argument count, so two Java methods
sharing a name and an argument count cannot both keep that name.
One of each group keeps it, chosen as the form most natural to call
from a script, and the rest are listed here under the name they were
given.

Suffixes come from parameter names where those tell the group apart,
and from parameter types where they do not.

Total renamed: 7820
